# Branch Review Ledger

Use this ledger to prevent repeated branch and PR reviews when the reviewed HEAD has not changed.

This file is a frozen historical table. New review records are immutable one-row files under `docs/branch-review-records/`, so simultaneous PRs never edit a shared hunk. Never rewrite an existing review record's content; append a correction or superseding record instead. The historical table and archives are retained for lookup, but normal PRs do not rotate, deduplicate, or add rows to them. The `merge` attribute is deliberately unspecified: GitHub cannot run a workstation-local merge driver.

`npm run check:ledger-write-discipline` rejects a dated-row change in this historical table. Equivalent retry records converge on the same content-addressed file; near-duplicates remain deliberate corrections and use `ledger:append --supersede`.

An already-open branch that contains a pre-system table row must run `npm run ledger:migrate-legacy -- --dry-run` and then `npm run ledger:migrate-legacy` before its next push. That preserves the row in an immutable file while removing the branch's shared-table hunk; commit both changes together. This is a one-time compatibility migration, not a normal review command.

Do not hand-edit this table or a record file. Read the whole corpus with `npm run ledger:lookup` and write new entries with `npm run ledger:append`; it creates one content-addressed file under `docs/branch-review-records/`.

## Lookup Procedure

Never scan the table by eye — it is over a thousand rows, and the reason repeat reviews used to slip through is that a recorded HEAD is often an abbreviation the reader's `git rev-parse` output does not literally equal.

```bash
npm run ledger:lookup -- <branch-or-ref> --scope "<scope keyword>"
```

It resolves the HEAD itself (`--head <sha>` overrides), matches abbreviated SHAs against full ones, normalizes `origin/x`, `` `x` ``, and `PR #123 / x` to the same ref, and prints one of two verdicts:

- **ALREADY REVIEWED** — summarize the prior outcome and skip the repeat pass unless the user explicitly asked for a fresh one.
- **NOT REVIEWED at this HEAD** — review the scope that changed, then append a record. Earlier records for the same ref at other HEADs are listed so you can scope the pass to the delta.

For branch-cleanup passes run the lookup before inspecting branch diffs, so unchanged reviewed branches are filtered out early. `npm run sweep:branch-ledger` does this for every remote branch at once.

## Recording a Review

```bash
npm run ledger:append -- --ref "PR #1234 / codex/my-branch" --head <full-40-char-sha> \
  --scope "<what was reviewed>" --outcome "<verdict + the findings that matter>" \
  --checks "<exact gates run, with results; say so explicitly if no provider-backed check ran>"
```

The six columns are fixed: **Date · Branch or ref · Reviewed HEAD · Scope · Outcome · Checks**. `append` stamps the date, resolves the HEAD to a full SHA, escapes prose pipes as `\|`, collapses newlines, writes UTF-8, and refuses an exact or ref/HEAD/scope duplicate (pass `--supersede` when a record deliberately replaces an earlier one).

Record the **Reviewed HEAD** as the full 40-character SHA. `see PR head`, `pending final head`, and 8-character abbreviations are what made the throttle unreliable: no lookup can match them, so the review runs again. When the head genuinely does not exist yet, write `n/a - <reason>` and append a real record once it does.

`npm run check:branch-review-ledger` (in `verify:full` and CI) enforces the `ledger` merge attribute, six cells per record, clean UTF-8 (no mojibake from a non-UTF-8 append), table-row records only, real dates, and no duplicates. Records dated 2026-07-29 or later must additionally carry a resolvable HEAD. The merge driver is installed by `npm install` / `hooks:install` (`merge.ledger.driver`). Archives under `docs/archive/branch-review-ledger-*.md` are checked for the same structural rules.

### Archival Policy

This historical table is frozen (`npm run check:ledger-write-discipline` rejects any dated-row edit or rotation here). All new review records are written as immutable content-addressed files under `docs/branch-review-records/` via `npm run ledger:append` (and indexed in `docs/branch-review-index.md` via `npm run ledger:index`). See [`docs/branch-review-archival-policy.md`](branch-review-archival-policy.md) for what may and may not be done to review records.

### Historical note — 2026-07-28 hygiene pass

Records before 2026-07-28 were written by hand and had drifted: 146 lines carried mojibake from a non-UTF-8 shell append, 46 rows were duplicates that the mojibake hid from the duplicate check, 21 rows had the wrong cell count, and 4 records were written as a heading plus bullet list inside the table, which split it. That pass restored 140 corrupted lines byte-exact from git history, repaired the remaining 6 separators, converted the 4 block records into rows, dropped the exact duplicates, and normalized every row to six cells. No record's content or verdict was changed, and no record was removed except exact duplicates — which this file's contract has always allowed.
